## 4. 免疫激活核心靶点数据集构建操作

### 4.1 操作目的

从免疫学专业数据库中系统收集与免疫激活（尤其是抗肿瘤免疫激活）相关的基因，构建"免疫激活核心靶点数据集"，用于与抗肿瘤靶点取交集，识别具有双重作用的靶点。

### 4.2 ImmPort数据库操作

**ImmPort（https://www.immport.org/）**是NIH资助的免疫学数据共享平台，提供经过人工策划的免疫相关基因列表。

1. 访问 https://www.immport.org/
2. 点击「Data」→「Shared Data」→「Search」
3. 点击「Gene List」→ 查找已整合的免疫基因列表
4. 或直接访问：https://www.immport.org/shared/genelists
5. 下载「Immuno Gene Set」：截至2025年，ImmPort数据库已收录**>6,000个经人工策划的免疫相关基因**（含免疫检查点、细胞因子、趋化因子、补体系统、先天免疫等全类别），较2018年版本（~2,266个）已大幅扩充（数据来源：ImmPort官方文档 https://www.immport.org/about，访问日期2025年3月）
6. 保存下载的CSV文件（含Gene Symbol、Entrez ID和功能注释）

> **数据库动态更新提示**：ImmPort基因列表持续更新，应在每次下载时记录访问日期版本号，并在论文方法部分标注"版本截止日期：YYYY-MM-DD"。

**ImmPort已整理的关键基因集（直接下载链接）**：
- 免疫检查点相关：`ImmPort_Gene_List_immune_checkpoint.csv`
- 细胞因子和受体：`ImmPort_Gene_List_cytokine.csv`
- 全部免疫基因：`ImmPort_Gene_List_all_immune.csv`

```r
immport_genes <- read.csv("ImmPort_Gene_List_all_immune.csv")
cat("ImmPort免疫基因总数:", nrow(immport_genes), "\n")
cat("列名:", paste(colnames(immport_genes), collapse=", "), "\n")
```

### 4.3 InnateDB数据库操作

**InnateDB（https://www.innatedb.com/）**提供先天免疫相关通路和基因数据：

1. 访问 https://www.innatedb.com/
2. 点击「Pathways」→ 浏览免疫相关通路（如「Toll-like receptor signaling pathway」「Type I interferon signaling pathway」）
3. 对于每个目标通路，点击「Gene List」导出该通路的基因
4. 或使用「Gene」搜索功能搜索特定免疫基因
5. 下载基因列表（TXT格式）

### 4.4 KEGG免疫通路靶点提取（R代码）

```r
library(KEGGREST)
library(tidyverse)

immune_pathways <- c(
  "hsa04660",  # T cell receptor signaling pathway（T细胞受体信号）
  "hsa04658",  # Th1 and Th2 cell differentiation
  "hsa04659",  # Th17 cell differentiation
  "hsa04661",  # B cell receptor signaling pathway（B细胞受体信号）
  "hsa04062",  # Chemokine signaling pathway（趋化因子信号）
  "hsa04064",  # NF-κB signaling pathway（NF-κB信号，免疫炎症）
  "hsa04630",  # JAK-STAT signaling pathway（JAK-STAT信号）
  "hsa04640",  # Hematopoietic cell lineage（造血细胞谱系）
  "hsa04612",  # Antigen processing and presentation（抗原提呈）
  "hsa04650",  # Natural killer cell mediated cytotoxicity（NK细胞毒性）
  "hsa04151",  # PI3K-Akt signaling pathway（与免疫细胞增殖相关）
  "hsa05200"   # Pathways in cancer（癌症相关通路，与免疫逃逸相关）
)

cat("正在从KEGG提取免疫通路基因...\n")
all_immune_genes <- data.frame()

for (pathway_id in immune_pathways) {
  tryCatch({
    pathway_info <- keggGet(pathway_id)[[1]]
    pathway_name <- pathway_info$NAME

    genes <- pathway_info$GENE
    if (!is.null(genes)) {
      gene_ids <- genes[seq(1, length(genes), by=2)]  # 奇数位置=KO/NCBI ID
      gene_symbols <- genes[seq(2, length(genes), by=2)]  # 偶数位置=Symbol+描述

      gene_sym_clean <- sapply(gene_symbols, function(g) {
        strsplit(g, ";")[[1]][1]
      })

      pathway_genes <- data.frame(
        PathwayID = pathway_id,
        PathwayName = pathway_name,
        GeneSymbol = gene_sym_clean,
        stringsAsFactors = FALSE
      )

      all_immune_genes <- rbind(all_immune_genes, pathway_genes)
      cat(pathway_id, "(", pathway_name, "):", nrow(pathway_genes), "个基因\n")
    }

    Sys.sleep(0.5)  # API速率限制

  }, error = function(e) {
    cat("⚠警告：", pathway_id, "查询失败：", e$message, "\n")
  })
}

kegg_immune_unique <- all_immune_genes %>%
  group_by(GeneSymbol) %>%
  summarise(
    Pathways = paste(unique(PathwayID), collapse="|"),
    PathwayNames = paste(unique(PathwayName), collapse="|"),
    n_pathways = n_distinct(PathwayID)
  )

cat("\n===== KEGG免疫通路基因汇总 =====\n")
cat("总基因数（去重后）:", nrow(kegg_immune_unique), "\n")
cat("参与≥2个免疫通路的基因数:", sum(kegg_immune_unique$n_pathways >= 2), "\n")

write.csv(kegg_immune_unique, "KEGG_immune_pathway_genes.csv", row.names=FALSE)
cat("\n结果已保存至 KEGG_immune_pathway_genes.csv\n")
```

## 5. 免疫检查点靶点纳入标准与基因列表

### 5.1 操作目的

建立免疫检查点相关基因的标准收录列表，确保研究涵盖当前免疫肿瘤学最重要的靶点。

### 5.2 核心免疫检查点基因列表

**抑制性免疫检查点（Inhibitory Checkpoints，优先研究目标）**：

| 基因Symbol | 蛋白名称 | NCBI Gene ID | 功能说明 | 研究优先级 |
|-----------|---------|-------------|---------|-----------|
| ***PDCD1*** | PD-1 | 5133 | T细胞抑制性受体，癌症免疫治疗核心靶点 | **最高** |
| ***CD274*** | PD-L1 | 29126 | PD-1的配体，肿瘤细胞免疫逃逸关键分子 | **最高** |
| ***PDCD1LG2*** | PD-L2 | 80380 | PD-1的另一配体 | 高 |
| ***CTLA4*** | CTLA-4 | 1493 | T细胞活化负调控，FDA批准靶点 | **最高** |
| ***HAVCR2*** | TIM-3 | 84868 | T细胞耗竭标志物，多种癌症高表达 | 高 |
| ***LAG3*** | LAG-3 | 3902 | 参与T细胞功能抑制，新兴靶点 | 高 |
| ***TIGIT*** | TIGIT | 201633 | NK细胞和T细胞的共抑制受体 | 高 |
| ***VSIR*** | VISTA | 64115 | 髓系细胞上的免疫检查点 | 中 |
| ***CD96*** | CD96 | 10225 | NK细胞上的抑制性受体 | 中 |
| ***BTLA*** | BTLA | 151888 | B和T淋巴细胞衰减因子 | 中 |

**共刺激分子（Co-stimulatory Molecules，促进免疫激活的靶点）**：

| 基因Symbol | 蛋白名称 | NCBI Gene ID | 功能说明 |
|-----------|---------|-------------|---------|
| ***CD28*** | CD28 | 940 | T细胞活化共刺激受体（B7的受体） |
| ***ICOS*** | ICOS | 29851 | 诱导型共刺激分子 |
| ***CD137*** | 4-1BB/CD137 | 2296 | T细胞活化共刺激分子 |
| ***CD27*** | CD27 | 939 | 肿瘤坏死因子受体家族成员 |
| ***OX40*** | OX40/CD134 | 7293 | TNF受体，T细胞增殖和存活调控 |
| ***TNFRSF18*** | GITR | 8784 | 糖皮质激素诱导的TNF受体 |

**配体分子**：

| 基因Symbol | 蛋白名称 | NCBI Gene ID |
|-----------|---------|-------------|
| ***CD80*** | B7-1/CD80 | 941 |
| ***CD86*** | B7-2/CD86 | 942 |
| ***CD40LG*** | CD40L | 959 |
| ***TNFSF4*** | OX40L | 7292 |

**整合输出：免疫检查点靶点完整列表**：

```r
immune_checkpoint_genes <- data.frame(
  GeneSymbol = c(
    "PDCD1", "CD274", "PDCD1LG2", "CTLA4", "HAVCR2", "LAG3", "TIGIT", "VSIR", "CD96", "BTLA",
    "CD28", "ICOS", "TNFRSF9", "CD27", "TNFRSF4", "TNFRSF18",
    "CD80", "CD86", "CD40LG", "TNFSF4"
  ),
  Category = c(
    rep("Inhibitory_Checkpoint", 10),
    rep("Co_stimulatory", 6),
    rep("Ligand", 4)
  ),
  Priority = c(
    rep("High", 4), rep("Medium", 6),  # 抑制性（4高+6中）
    rep("Medium", 6),                   # 共刺激（全中）
    rep("Medium", 4)                    # 配体（全中）
  ),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

cat("免疫检查点靶点列表：", nrow(immune_checkpoint_genes), "个基因\n")
write.csv(immune_checkpoint_genes, "immune_checkpoint_gene_list.csv", row.names=FALSE)
```

## 7. 韦恩图分析R代码

### 7.1 操作目的

通过韦恩图（Venn Diagram）可视化分析"中药化合物预测靶点"与"抗肿瘤靶点"和"免疫激活靶点"三组数据的交集，识别同时满足多个条件的"双功能"或"三功能"核心靶点。

### 7.2 完整韦恩图代码

```r
library(VennDiagram)
library(ggvenn)
library(ggplot2)
library(tidyverse)

tcm_targets <- read.csv("TCM_targets_integrated.csv")
tcm_gene_set <- unique(toupper(trimws(tcm_targets$Gene_Symbol)))
tcm_gene_set <- tcm_gene_set[!is.na(tcm_gene_set) & tcm_gene_set != ""]

tumor_targets_df <- read.csv("GSE53819_significant_DEGs.csv")
tumor_gene_set <- unique(toupper(trimws(tumor_targets_df$GeneSymbol)))

immune_immport <- read.csv("ImmPort_Gene_List_all_immune.csv")
immune_kegg <- read.csv("KEGG_immune_pathway_genes.csv")
immune_checkpoint <- read.csv("immune_checkpoint_gene_list.csv")

immune_gene_set <- unique(c(
  toupper(trimws(immune_immport$gene_symbol)),
  toupper(trimws(immune_kegg$GeneSymbol)),
  toupper(trimws(immune_checkpoint$GeneSymbol))
))
immune_gene_set <- immune_gene_set[!is.na(immune_gene_set) & immune_gene_set != ""]

cat("===== 靶点数量统计 =====\n")
cat("中药化合物靶点（TCM）:", length(tcm_gene_set), "个\n")
cat("抗肿瘤靶点（Tumor）:", length(tumor_gene_set), "个\n")
cat("免疫激活靶点（Immune）:", length(immune_gene_set), "个\n")

tcm_tumor_overlap <- intersect(tcm_gene_set, tumor_gene_set)
cat("\nTCM ∩ Tumor:", length(tcm_tumor_overlap), "个基因\n")

tcm_immune_overlap <- intersect(tcm_gene_set, immune_gene_set)
cat("TCM ∩ Immune:", length(tcm_immune_overlap), "个基因\n")

tumor_immune_overlap <- intersect(tumor_gene_set, immune_gene_set)
cat("Tumor ∩ Immune:", length(tumor_immune_overlap), "个基因\n")

core_targets <- Reduce(intersect, list(tcm_gene_set, tumor_gene_set, immune_gene_set))
cat("\n===== 核心靶点（三者交集）:", length(core_targets), "个 =====\n")
print(sort(core_targets))

venn_data <- list(
  "TCM靶点" = tcm_gene_set,
  "抗肿瘤靶点" = tumor_gene_set,
  "免疫激活靶点" = immune_gene_set
)

venn_plot <- ggvenn(
  venn_data,
  fill_color = c("#4472C4", "#ED7D31", "#70AD47"),
  fill_alpha = 0.4,
  stroke_size = 0.5,
  set_name_size = 4.5,
  text_size = 3.5,
  show_percentage = FALSE  # 显示绝对数字而非百分比
) +
  ggtitle("中药化合物靶点 × 抗肿瘤靶点 × 免疫激活靶点 韦恩图分析") +
  theme(plot.title = element_text(hjust=0.5, size=14, face="bold"))

ggsave("target_venn_diagram.pdf", venn_plot, width=10, height=8, dpi=300)
ggsave("target_venn_diagram.png", venn_plot, width=10, height=8, dpi=300)
cat("\n韦恩图已保存至 target_venn_diagram.pdf/png\n")

pdf("target_venn_detailed.pdf", width=10, height=8)
venn.diagram(
  x = venn_data,
  filename = NULL,
  category.names = c("中药靶点\n(n=%d)" %>% sprintf(length(tcm_gene_set)),
                     "抗肿瘤靶点\n(n=%d)" %>% sprintf(length(tumor_gene_set)),
                     "免疫激活靶点\n(n=%d)" %>% sprintf(length(immune_gene_set))),
  fill = c("#4472C4", "#ED7D31", "#70AD47"),
  alpha = 0.4,
  col = "white",
  cex = 2,
  fontface = "bold",
  cat.cex = 1.2,
  cat.fontface = "bold",
  cat.dist = c(0.03, 0.03, 0.03),
  margin = 0.05,
  main = "靶点韦恩图分析",
  main.cex = 1.5
)
dev.off()

core_target_df <- data.frame(
  GeneSymbol = core_targets,
  InTCM = TRUE,
  InTumor = TRUE,
  InImmune = TRUE,
  TargetType = "Core_Triple_Overlap"  # 三功能靶点
)

core_target_df$IsCheckpoint <- core_target_df$GeneSymbol %in%
  toupper(immune_checkpoint$GeneSymbol)

core_target_df <- core_target_df %>%
  arrange(desc(IsCheckpoint), GeneSymbol)

write.csv(core_target_df, "core_targets_triple_overlap.csv", row.names=FALSE)

cat("\n核心靶点信息已保存至 core_targets_triple_overlap.csv\n")
cat("免疫检查点核心靶点:", sum(core_target_df$IsCheckpoint), "个\n")
cat("其他核心靶点:", sum(!core_target_df$IsCheckpoint), "个\n")

if (length(core_targets) < 5) {
  cat("\n⚠警告：核心三功能靶点数量不足5个，请检查以下可能原因：\n")
  cat("1. 中药化合物靶点数量不足（须确保TCM靶点≥100个）\n")
  cat("2. 抗肿瘤靶点过滤标准过严（可尝试放宽adj.p至0.1）\n")
  cat("3. 免疫激活靶点库范围过窄（可扩大至更多KEGG通路）\n")
  cat("4. 可接受方案：将「TCM ∩ Tumor」和「TCM ∩ Immune」分别作为独立靶点集使用\n")
}
```

---

## 8. 靶点数据集标准化与注释规范

### 8.1 操作目的

将来自不同数据库的靶点统一转换为标准化格式（HGNC官方基因Symbol + NCBI Gene ID），解决不同数据库间的基因标识符差异问题。

### 8.2 使用biomaRt包进行ID转换

```r
library(biomaRt)
library(tidyverse)


mart <- useMart(biomart = "ensembl",
                dataset = "hsapiens_gene_ensembl")


ensembl_ids <- c("ENSG00000141510", "ENSG00000012048", "ENSG00000136997")  # 示例

symbol_result <- getBM(
  attributes = c("ensembl_gene_id", "hgnc_symbol", "entrezgene_id", "description"),
  filters = "ensembl_gene_id",
  values = ensembl_ids,
  mart = mart
)
print(symbol_result)

gene_symbols_raw <- c("TP53", "EGFR", "AKT1", "PDCD1", "CD274")  # 示例

entrez_result <- getBM(
  attributes = c("hgnc_symbol", "entrezgene_id", "chromosome_name",
                  "start_position", "end_position", "description"),
  filters = "hgnc_symbol",
  values = gene_symbols_raw,
  mart = mart
)
print(entrez_result)

all_targets_raw <- read.csv("all_targets_combined_raw.csv")
gene_symbols_to_convert <- unique(toupper(trimws(all_targets_raw$GeneSymbol)))

cat("正在进行批量ID转换（共", length(gene_symbols_to_convert), "个基因）...\n")
id_conversion_table <- getBM(
  attributes = c("hgnc_symbol", "entrezgene_id", "ensembl_gene_id",
                  "uniprot_gn_id", "chromosome_name", "description"),
  filters = "hgnc_symbol",
  values = gene_symbols_to_convert,
  mart = mart
)

id_table_dedup <- id_conversion_table %>%
  group_by(hgnc_symbol) %>%
  summarise(
    entrezgene_id = first(na.omit(entrezgene_id)),
    ensembl_gene_id = first(na.omit(ensembl_gene_id)),
    uniprot_id = first(na.omit(uniprot_gn_id)),
    chromosome = first(na.omit(chromosome_name)),
    description = first(na.omit(description))
  )

failed_genes <- setdiff(gene_symbols_to_convert, id_table_dedup$hgnc_symbol)
if (length(failed_genes) > 0) {
  cat("⚠警告：以下基因Symbol在Ensembl中未找到（可能是过时或错误的Symbol）:\n")
  cat(paste(failed_genes, collapse=", "), "\n")
  cat("这些基因需要手动在HGNC数据库（https://www.genenames.org/）中核查\n")
}

cat("ID转换成功:", nrow(id_table_dedup), "/", length(gene_symbols_to_convert), "\n")

all_targets_annotated <- all_targets_raw %>%
  mutate(GeneSymbol_upper = toupper(trimws(GeneSymbol))) %>%
  left_join(id_table_dedup, by = c("GeneSymbol_upper" = "hgnc_symbol"))

write.csv(all_targets_annotated, "target_dataset_standardized.csv", row.names=FALSE)
cat("标准化靶点数据集已保存：", nrow(all_targets_annotated), "条记录\n")

cat("\n===== 最终靶点数据集汇总 =====\n")
cat("总靶点数:", n_distinct(all_targets_annotated$GeneSymbol_upper), "个\n")
cat("有EntrezGene ID:", sum(!is.na(all_targets_annotated$entrezgene_id)), "个\n")
cat("有UniProt ID:", sum(!is.na(all_targets_annotated$uniprot_id)), "个\n")
```

### 8.3 质控标准

- **[必须]** 所有基因须使用HGNC官方Symbol（非旧版Symbol），通过biomaRt进行验证
- **[必须]** 靶点转换成功率须≥90%（若<90%须手动核查失败基因）
- **[必须]** 最终输出的标准化靶点数据集须包含：HGNC Symbol、EntrezGene ID、UniProt ID

---

## 9. 质控标准与避坑指南（模块03总览）

### 9.1 模块03完成标志

在进入模块04前，须确认以下项目：

- [ ] 抗肿瘤靶点数据集已建立（来自GEO差异分析 + GeneCards + OMIM + DisGeNET）
  - 靶点数量：**100-800个**（根据选择的瘤种和数据集数量）
  - 每个靶点须有数据来源标注
- [ ] 免疫激活靶点数据集已建立（来自ImmPort + KEGG免疫通路 + 免疫检查点列表）
  - 靶点数量：**200-1000个**
- [ ] 韦恩图分析已完成
  - 核心三功能靶点（TCM ∩ Tumor ∩ Immune）数量：**≥10个**（理想≥30个）
  - 韦恩图PDF文件已输出
- [ ] 所有靶点已完成标准化（HGNC Symbol + Gene ID转换）
  - 转换成功率≥90%
- [ ] 最终核心靶点数据集（传递给模块04）已准备好

### 9.2 靶点数量范围判断指南

| 靶点类型 | 过少（须回溯）| 正常范围 | 过多（须过滤）|
|---------|-------------|---------|-------------|
| TCM化合物靶点 | <50个 | 100-600个 | >1000个 |
| 抗肿瘤靶点（单数据集）| <100个 | 200-800个 | >2000个 |
| 免疫激活靶点 | <100个 | 300-1000个 | >2000个 |
| 核心三功能靶点 | <5个 | 10-100个 | >200个 |

### 9.3 避坑指南汇总

> **陷阱1**：直接使用GeneCards的"Relevance Score"排名前20的基因作为靶点集，不进行DEA验证
> **规避方法**：GeneCards的评分基于文献频次，而非直接的实验证据。须将GeneCards结果与GEO/TCGA差异分析结果取交集，只有在两种方法中均出现的基因才可作为高可信度靶点。

> **陷阱2**：韦恩图中核心三功能靶点过少（<5个），报告时回避这一问题
> **规避方法**：核心靶点<5个是可以接受的特殊情况，但须在论文中说明原因（例如：该中药的化合物靶点与该瘤种的差异基因重叠较少，体现了其可能的新颖机制）。可同时报告双功能靶点（TCM ∩ Tumor，或TCM ∩ Immune），增加分析深度。

> **陷阱3**：KEGG数据库查询失败（网络连接问题）
> **解决方案**：(1) 检查网络连接；(2) 使用KEGG数据库的镜像站（部分学校服务器可能屏蔽了外网）；(3) 直接从KEGG网站手动下载目标通路的基因列表（https://www.kegg.jp/pathway/hsa04660 → 右上角「Gene」按钮）；(4) 使用R包的本地KEGG数据（`KEGG.db`包，虽然更新不及时，但在网络问题时可用）。

---

*本模块结束 | 下一模块：模块04 网络药理学与分子对接虚拟筛选*

# 第七章　网络药理学核心分析标准化操作全流程

*Chapter 7: Network Pharmacology Analysis: Full Standard Operating Procedure*


## 1 模块概述与流程图

### 1.1 本模块目标

本模块系统介绍中药活性化合物抗肿瘤靶点的计算机辅助虚拟筛选完整流程，涵盖网络药理学分析、富集分析和分子对接三大核心技术。操作规范严格按照国际同行评审标准制定，确保结果的可重复性和可发表性。

**核心筛选标准：**
- 活性成分：**OB≥30%**，**DL≥0.18**（TCMSP数据库标准）
- Hub靶点：**MCC算法前20名**
- 分子对接阈值：结合自由能 **≤-7.0 kcal/mol**
- 富集分析显著性：**p.adjust<0.05**（BH法），**q<0.2**

### 1.2 整体分析流程

```
中药材 → TCMSP数据库 → 活性成分筛选(OB≥30%, DL≥0.18)
    ↓
活性成分靶点预测 → Swiss Target Prediction / TCMSP靶点
    ↓
疾病靶点获取 → GeneCards/OMIM/DisGeNET
    ↓
靶点取交集（韦恩图）→ 候选靶点集
    ↓
PPI网络构建（STRING，置信度≥0.7）
    ↓
Cytoscape可视化 + CytoHubba(MCC)筛选Hub靶点
    ↓
KEGG/GO富集分析 → 关键通路确认
    ↓
分子对接（AutoDock Vina 1.2.3）→ 结合自由能筛选
    ↓
PyMOL可视化 → 关键互作分析
    ↓
候选化合物-靶点优先级排序
```

### 1.3 软件环境准备

#### 1.3.1 必需软件清单

| 软件/包 | 版本 | 用途 | 下载地址 |
|---------|------|------|----------|
| R | 4.3.3 | 统计分析核心环境 | https://cran.r-project.org |
| RStudio | 2024.09.0 | R集成开发环境 | https://posit.co/rstudio |
| Cytoscape | 3.10.1 | 网络可视化 | https://cytoscape.org |
| AutoDock Vina | 1.2.3 | 分子对接 | https://vina.scripps.edu |
| PyMOL | 2.5 | 蛋白结构可视化 | https://pymol.org |
| OpenBabel | 3.1.1 | 分子格式转换 | https://openbabel.org |
| MGLTools | 1.5.7 | AutoDock辅助工具 | http://mgltools.scripps.edu |

#### 1.3.2 R包安装代码

```r

options(repos = c(CRAN = "https://mirrors.ustc.edu.cn/CRAN/"))

if (!requireNamespace("BiocManager", quietly = TRUE)) {
  install.packages("BiocManager")
}
BiocManager::install(version = "3.18")  # 对应R 4.3.x

BiocManager::install(c(
  "clusterProfiler",   # 版本 4.10.1
  "org.Hs.eg.db",      # 人类基因组注释数据库
  "pathview",          # KEGG通路可视化
  "enrichplot",        # 富集分析可视化
  "DOSE",              # 疾病本体富集
  "TCGAbiolinks",      # TCGA数据获取
  "DESeq2",            # 差异表达分析
  "edgeR",             # 差异表达（备选）
  "limma"              # 微阵列/RNA-seq分析
), update = FALSE)

install.packages(c(
  "ggplot2",           # 版本 3.4.4 绘图
  "ggrepel",           # 标签不重叠
  "igraph",            # 网络分析
  "tidyverse",         # 数据处理套件

  "VennDiagram",       # 韦恩图
  "RColorBrewer",      # 配色方案
  "cowplot",           # 图形组合
  "survminer",         # 生存分析可视化
  "survival",          # Cox回归
  "ggpubr",            # 出版级图形
  "stringr",           # 字符串处理
  "dplyr",             # 数据操作
  "readxl",            # 读取Excel
  "openxlsx"           # 写入Excel
))

pkg_check <- c("clusterProfiler", "org.Hs.eg.db", "pathview",
               "ggplot2", "igraph", "survival", "survminer")
for (pkg in pkg_check) {
  if (requireNamespace(pkg, quietly = TRUE)) {
    cat(sprintf("OK: %s 安装成功\n", pkg))
  } else {
    cat(sprintf("FAIL: %s 安装失败，请重试\n", pkg))
  }
}
```

---

## 2 网络药理学分析

### 2.1 中药活性成分与靶点获取

#### 2.1.1 TCMSP数据库检索（点击级操作）

1. 打开浏览器（推荐Chrome 120+），访问 https://old.tcmsp-e.com/tcmsp.php
2. 在搜索框中输入中药名称（如"黄芪"），点击"Search"按钮
3. 在结果页面，点击"Ingredients"标签，查看所有化合物
4. **关键参数筛选：**
   - 点击列标题"OB(%)"进行排序
   - 筛选 **OB≥30%** 的化合物（口服生物利用度）
   - 同时筛选 **DL≥0.18**（药物相似性）
5. 点击筛选后化合物名称，进入该化合物详情页
6. 点击"Related Targets"查看靶点信息
7. 右键→"另存为"，下载CSV格式数据

> ⚠ **注意事项：** TCMSP数据库有时访问较慢，建议在非高峰时段（早8点前或晚10点后）操作。若网站无法访问，可使用 https://tcmsp-e.com/tcmsp.php 备用地址，或下载BATMAN-TCM（http://bionet.ncpsb.org.cn/batman-tcm/）作为补充数据源。

#### 2.1.2 靶点数据整理R代码

```r

library(tidyverse)
library(readxl)

tcmsp_data <- read.csv("huangqi_ingredients.csv",
                        header = TRUE,
                        stringsAsFactors = FALSE,
                        fileEncoding = "UTF-8")

active_compounds <- tcmsp_data %>%
  filter(OB >= 30, DL >= 0.18) %>%
  select(mol_id, molecule_name, OB, DL)

cat("筛选后活性成分数量：", nrow(active_compounds), "\n")

targets_data <- read.csv("huangqi_targets.csv",
                          header = TRUE,
                          stringsAsFactors = FALSE)

compound_target <- targets_data %>%
  filter(mol_id %in% active_compounds$mol_id) %>%
  left_join(active_compounds, by = "mol_id") %>%
  select(mol_id, molecule_name, target_name, UniProtKB) %>%
  distinct()

target_genes <- unique(compound_target$target_name)
cat("唯一靶点基因数量：", length(target_genes), "\n")

write.csv(compound_target, "compound_target_pairs.csv",
          row.names = FALSE,
          fileEncoding = "UTF-8")
write.csv(data.frame(gene = target_genes), "target_genes.csv",
          row.names = FALSE)
```

#### 2.1.3 疾病靶点获取（GeneCards操作步骤）

1. 访问 https://www.genecards.org/
2. 在搜索框中输入疾病名称（如"nasopharyngeal carcinoma"）
3. 在结果页面，点击"Diseases"标签
4. 找到目标疾病条目，点击进入疾病详情页
5. 点击"Genes"标签，查看相关基因列表
6. 设置筛选条件：**Relevance Score ≥ 10**（中等关联以上）
7. 点击页面右上角"Export"→"CSV"导出基因列表

> ⚠ **注意事项：** GeneCards需要注册账号才能下载完整数据（免费注册）。建议同时从OMIM（https://omim.org）和DisGeNET（https://www.disgenet.org）补充疾病靶点，以提高覆盖度。

### 2.2 靶点交集分析（韦恩图）

```r

library(VennDiagram)
library(RColorBrewer)

compound_targets <- read.csv("target_genes.csv")$gene
disease_targets <- read.csv("disease_genes_NPC.csv")$gene  # 鼻咽癌靶点

compound_targets <- toupper(trimws(compound_targets))
disease_targets <- toupper(trimws(disease_targets))

intersection_targets <- intersect(compound_targets, disease_targets)
cat("化合物靶点数：", length(compound_targets), "\n")
cat("疾病靶点数：", length(disease_targets), "\n")
cat("交集靶点数：", length(intersection_targets), "\n")

venn.diagram(
  x = list(
    "化合物靶点" = compound_targets,
    "疾病靶点(NPC)" = disease_targets
  ),
  filename = "venn_compound_disease.png",
  output = TRUE,
  imagetype = "png",
  resolution = 300,
  compression = "lzw",
  height = 3000,
  width = 3000,
  units = "px",
  col = "black",
  fill = c("#4DBBD5", "#E64B35"),
  alpha = 0.5,
  cex = 2.5,
  fontfamily = "sans",
  cat.cex = 2,
  cat.fontfamily = "sans",
  cat.col = c("#4DBBD5", "#E64B35"),
  margin = 0.05
)

write.csv(data.frame(gene = intersection_targets),
          "intersection_targets.csv",
          row.names = FALSE)
```

### 2.3 PPI网络构建（STRING数据库）

#### 2.3.1 STRING数据库操作步骤（点击级）

1. 访问 https://string-db.org/
2. 点击主页"Multiple Proteins"标签（因为有多个靶点）
3. 在文本框中粘贴交集靶点基因列表（每行一个基因名）
4. 在"Organism"下拉菜单中选择"Homo sapiens"（人类）
5. 点击"Search"按钮
6. 在结果页面，点击右侧面板"Settings"，**根据分析阶段设置不同置信度阈值**：

   | 分析阶段 | 置信度设置 | STRING分值 | 用途 |
   |---------|----------|-----------|------|
   | **初步发现阶段（Discovery）** | 中等置信度 | **≥0.400（medium）** | 构建较大规模网络，不遗漏潜在关联 |
   | **核心靶点筛选（Core Analysis）** | 高置信度 | **≥0.700（high）** | 仅保留高可信互作，用于Hub靶点确定 |
   | **结果验证（Validation）** | 最高置信度 | ≥0.900（highest）| 验证核心Hub节点间的互作可靠性 |

   > **操作指引**：本手册推荐双层次分析策略：**先以≥0.400构建全景PPI网络**（确保覆盖度），**再以≥0.700筛选核心子网络**（确保准确度），两个网络均需导出并在论文中分别呈现（Supplementary Figure 和 Main Figure）。此双阈值策略参考 Szklarczyk et al. (2021, NAR, DOI: 10.1093/nar/gkaa1074) 官方推荐的置信度分级标准。

   - Network type 选择 "full STRING network"
   - 勾选 "Textmining"、"Experiments"、"Databases"、"Co-expression"
7. 点击"Update"刷新网络
8. 点击页面下方"Exports"标签：
   - 下载"TSV"格式的互作数据（protein1, protein2, combined_score）
   - 点击"SVG"下载矢量图（可选）

#### 2.3.2 Cytoscape 3.10.1 安装与操作

**安装步骤：**
1. 访问 https://cytoscape.org/download.html
2. 选择对应操作系统版本（Windows/macOS/Linux），点击"Cytoscape_3_10_1_windows_64bit.exe"（以Windows为例）
3. 双击安装文件，按提示完成安装（需要Java 11+环境）
4. 启动Cytoscape，首次启动约需30-60秒

**CytoHubba插件安装：**
1. 打开Cytoscape，点击菜单栏"Apps" → "App Manager"
2. 在搜索框中输入"CytoHubba"
3. 在结果列表中找到"cytoHubba"，点击"Install"按钮
4. 等待安装完成（约1-2分钟），点击"Close"

**导入STRING网络数据：**
1. 点击菜单"File" → "Import" → "Network from File"
2. 选择从STRING下载的TSV文件
3. 在弹出的对话框中：
   - 将第1列（protein1）设置为"Source Node"（点击列标题，选择Source）
   - 将第2列（protein2）设置为"Target Node"
   - 将第3列（combined_score）设置为"Edge Attribute"（命名为"score"）
4. 点击"OK"导入网络

**网络布局调整：**
1. 点击菜单"Layout" → "Prefuse Force Directed Layout"（推荐用于PPI网络）
2. 等待布局计算完成
3. 点击"View" → "Fit Content"（快捷键Ctrl+Shift+F）使网络全屏显示

#### 2.3.3 CytoHubba MCC算法筛选Hub靶点（点击级）

1. 确认已激活含靶点的网络
2. 点击菜单"Apps" → "cytoHubba" → "Hub Objects"
3. 在弹出的CytoHubba面板中：
   - "Method"下拉菜单选择 **"MCC"**（Maximal Clique Centrality）
   - "Number of top objects"输入框填写 **"20"**
   - 点击"Submit"按钮
4. 等待计算完成（视网络大小，约10秒-2分钟）
5. 结果将在主网络中以颜色梯度（红色=最高分）标注Hub节点
6. 在"Results"面板中查看MCC得分排名
7. 导出结果：点击"Export Results" → 保存为CSV文件

> ⚠ **注意事项：** MCC算法计算量较大，节点数超过200个时可能需要等待5分钟以上。若网络过大，建议先用"Minimum Edges Per Node"过滤（推荐≥2）减少节点数量。

#### 2.3.4 网络参数分析R代码

```r

library(igraph)
library(ggplot2)
library(dplyr)

ppi_data <- read.table("string_interactions.tsv",
                        header = TRUE,
                        sep = "\t",
                        stringsAsFactors = FALSE)

ppi_filtered <- ppi_data %>%
  filter(combined_score >= 700)

g <- graph_from_data_frame(
  d = ppi_filtered[, c("protein1", "protein2")],
  directed = FALSE
)

degree_vals <- degree(g)

betweenness_vals <- betweenness(g, normalized = TRUE)

closeness_vals <- closeness(g, normalized = TRUE)

eigen_vals <- eigen_centrality(g)$vector

network_params <- data.frame(
  gene = V(g)$name,
  degree = degree_vals,
  betweenness = betweenness_vals,
  closeness = closeness_vals,
  eigenvector = eigen_vals
) %>%
  arrange(desc(degree))

cat("=== 网络拓扑统计 ===\n")
cat("节点数：", vcount(g), "\n")
cat("边数：", ecount(g), "\n")
cat("平均路径长度：", round(mean_distance(g), 3), "\n")
cat("网络密度：", round(edge_density(g), 4), "\n")
cat("聚类系数：", round(transitivity(g, type = "global"), 4), "\n")

mean_degree <- mean(network_params$degree)
sd_degree <- sd(network_params$degree)
mean_betweenness <- mean(network_params$betweenness)

hub_targets <- network_params %>%
  filter(degree > (mean_degree + sd_degree),
         betweenness > mean_betweenness) %>%
  arrange(desc(degree))

cat("\n=== Hub靶点（度值筛选）===\n")
print(head(hub_targets, 20))

write.csv(network_params, "ppi_network_parameters.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(hub_targets, "hub_targets_degree_filter.csv", row.names = FALSE)

ggplot(network_params, aes(x = degree)) +
  geom_histogram(bins = 30, fill = "#4DBBD5", color = "white", alpha = 0.8) +
  geom_vline(xintercept = mean_degree + sd_degree,
             color = "red", linetype = "dashed", linewidth = 1) +
  labs(title = "PPI网络度值分布",
       x = "度值 (Degree)",
       y = "节点数量",
       caption = "红色虚线：均值+1SD（Hub靶点筛选阈值）") +
  theme_classic(base_size = 14) +
  theme(plot.title = element_text(hjust = 0.5))

ggsave("ppi_degree_distribution.pdf", width = 8, height = 6, dpi = 300)
```

### 2.4 化合物-靶点-通路（C-T-P）网络构建

#### 2.4.1 C-T-P网络R代码

```r

library(igraph)
library(ggraph)
library(tidygraph)
library(dplyr)

compound_target <- read.csv("compound_target_pairs.csv")
hub_targets <- read.csv("hub_targets_degree_filter.csv")
kegg_results <- read.csv("kegg_enrichment_results.csv")

ct_pairs <- compound_target %>%
  filter(target_name %in% hub_targets$gene[1:20]) %>%
  select(molecule_name, target_name) %>%
  distinct()

top_pathways <- kegg_results %>%
  filter(p.adjust < 0.05) %>%
  arrange(p.adjust) %>%
  head(10)

library(tidyr)
target_pathway <- top_pathways %>%
  select(Description, geneID) %>%
  separate_rows(geneID, sep = "/") %>%
  rename(entrez_id = geneID, pathway = Description)

library(org.Hs.eg.db)
entrez_to_gene <- AnnotationDbi::select(
  org.Hs.eg.db,
  keys = unique(target_pathway$entrez_id),
  columns = c("ENTREZID", "SYMBOL"),
  keytype = "ENTREZID"
)

target_pathway <- target_pathway %>%
  left_join(entrez_to_gene, by = c("entrez_id" = "ENTREZID")) %>%
  filter(!is.na(SYMBOL)) %>%
  select(SYMBOL, pathway) %>%
  rename(target_name = SYMBOL)

edges_ct <- ct_pairs %>%
  rename(from = molecule_name, to = target_name) %>%
  mutate(type = "化合物-靶点")

edges_tp <- target_pathway %>%
  filter(target_name %in% hub_targets$gene[1:20]) %>%
  rename(from = target_name, to = pathway) %>%
  mutate(type = "靶点-通路")

all_edges <- bind_rows(edges_ct, edges_tp)

all_nodes <- data.frame(
  name = unique(c(all_edges$from, all_edges$to)),
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
) %>%
  mutate(
    node_type = case_when(
      name %in% ct_pairs$molecule_name ~ "化合物",
      name %in% hub_targets$gene[1:20] ~ "靶点",
      TRUE ~ "通路"
    )
  )

g_ctp <- tbl_graph(
  nodes = all_nodes,
  edges = all_edges %>%
    mutate(from_idx = match(from, all_nodes$name),
           to_idx = match(to, all_nodes$name)) %>%
    select(from = from_idx, to = to_idx, type),
  directed = FALSE
)

p_ctp <- ggraph(g_ctp, layout = "fr") +
  geom_edge_link(aes(color = type), alpha = 0.6, linewidth = 0.5) +
  geom_node_point(aes(color = node_type, size = node_type), alpha = 0.9) +
  geom_node_text(aes(label = name, color = node_type),
                 size = 2.5, repel = TRUE, max.overlaps = 30) +
  scale_color_manual(
    values = c("化合物" = "#E64B35", "靶点" = "#4DBBD5", "通路" = "#00A087"),
    name = "节点类型"
  ) +
  scale_size_manual(values = c("化合物" = 5, "靶点" = 7, "通路" = 6)) +
  scale_edge_color_manual(
    values = c("化合物-靶点" = "#7B4F71", "靶点-通路" = "#91A8A4"),
    name = "连接类型"
  ) +
  labs(title = "化合物-靶点-通路(C-T-P)三元网络") +
  theme_graph(base_size = 12) +
  theme(plot.title = element_text(hjust = 0.5, size = 14, face = "bold"))

ggsave("CTP_network.pdf", plot = p_ctp, width = 14, height = 12, dpi = 300)
ggsave("CTP_network.png", plot = p_ctp, width = 14, height = 12, dpi = 300)

cat("C-T-P网络已保存为PDF和PNG格式\n")
```

---

## 3 KEGG/GO富集分析

### 3.1 分析前准备

#### 3.1.1 基因ID转换

```r

library(clusterProfiler)
library(org.Hs.eg.db)
library(dplyr)

intersection_genes <- read.csv("intersection_targets.csv")$gene

gene_id_mapping <- bitr(
  geneID = intersection_genes,
  fromType = "SYMBOL",
  toType = c("ENTREZID", "ENSEMBL"),
  OrgDb = org.Hs.eg.db
)

cat("原始基因数：", length(intersection_genes), "\n")
cat("成功转换数：", nrow(gene_id_mapping), "\n")
cat("转换失败数：", length(intersection_genes) - nrow(gene_id_mapping), "\n")

failed_genes <- setdiff(intersection_genes, gene_id_mapping$SYMBOL)
if (length(failed_genes) > 0) {
  cat("转换失败的基因（请在NCBI Gene查询官方名称）：\n")
  print(failed_genes)
}

entrez_ids <- gene_id_mapping$ENTREZID
```

### 3.2 KEGG通路富集分析

```r

library(clusterProfiler)
library(enrichplot)
library(ggplot2)

kegg_result <- enrichKEGG(
  gene = entrez_ids,
  organism = "hsa",          # 人类
  pvalueCutoff = 0.05,
  pAdjustMethod = "BH",      # Benjamini-Hochberg校正
  qvalueCutoff = 0.2,
  minGSSize = 10,             # 最小通路基因数
  maxGSSize = 500             # 最大通路基因数
)

cat("显著富集KEGG通路数（p.adjust<0.05）：",
    nrow(subset(kegg_result@result, p.adjust < 0.05)), "\n")

kegg_significant <- kegg_result@result %>%
  filter(p.adjust < 0.05, qvalue < 0.2) %>%
  arrange(p.adjust)

write.csv(kegg_significant, "kegg_enrichment_results.csv", row.names = FALSE)

p_kegg_dot <- dotplot(
  kegg_result,
  showCategory = 20,
  orderBy = "GeneRatio",
  color = "p.adjust",
  font.size = 11
) +
  scale_color_gradient(
    low = "#E64B35",
    high = "#4DBBD5",
    name = "p.adjust"
  ) +
  labs(
    title = "KEGG通路富集分析气泡图",
    x = "GeneRatio",
    y = "KEGG通路"
  ) +
  theme(
    plot.title = element_text(hjust = 0.5, size = 14, face = "bold"),
    axis.text.y = element_text(size = 10)
  )

ggsave("kegg_dotplot.pdf", plot = p_kegg_dot, width = 12, height = 10, dpi = 300)

p_kegg_cnet <- cnetplot(
  kegg_result,
  showCategory = 5,
  foldChange = NULL,
  circular = FALSE,
  colorEdge = TRUE,
  node_label = "all"
)

ggsave("kegg_cnetplot.pdf", plot = p_kegg_cnet, width = 14, height = 12, dpi = 300)
```

```r
go_bp <- enrichGO(

cat("=== GO富集分析结果统计 ===\n")
cat("BP显著条目数：", nrow(subset(go_bp@result, p.adjust < 0.05)), "\n")
cat("MF显著条目数：", nrow(subset(go_mf@result, p.adjust < 0.05)), "\n")
cat("CC显著条目数：", nrow(subset(go_cc@result, p.adjust < 0.05)), "\n")

plot_go_bubble <- function(go_obj, title_text, n = 10) {
  df <- go_obj@result %>%
    filter(p.adjust < 0.05) %>%
    arrange(p.adjust) %>%
    head(n) %>%
    mutate(
      Description = factor(Description, levels = rev(Description)),
      GeneRatio_num = sapply(GeneRatio, function(x) {
        parts <- strsplit(x, "/")[[1]]
        as.numeric(parts[1]) / as.numeric(parts[2])
      })
    )

  ggplot(df, aes(x = GeneRatio_num, y = Description,
                  color = p.adjust, size = Count)) +
    geom_point() +
    scale_color_gradient(low = "#E64B35", high = "#4DBBD5",
                         name = "p.adjust") +
    scale_size_continuous(name = "基因数", range = c(3, 10)) +
    labs(title = title_text, x = "GeneRatio", y = NULL) +
    theme_classic(base_size = 11) +
    theme(
      plot.title = element_text(hjust = 0.5, face = "bold"),
      axis.text.y = element_text(size = 9)
    )
}

p_bp <- plot_go_bubble(go_bp, "GO-BP 生物过程")
p_mf <- plot_go_bubble(go_mf, "GO-MF 分子功能")
p_cc <- plot_go_bubble(go_cc, "GO-CC 细胞组分")

p_go_combined <- plot_grid(p_bp, p_mf, p_cc,
                            ncol = 1,
                            labels = c("A", "B", "C"),
                            label_size = 14,
                            rel_heights = c(1.2, 0.8, 0.8))

ggsave("GO_enrichment_combined.pdf", plot = p_go_combined,
       width = 12, height = 24, dpi = 300)

write.csv(go_bp@result, "go_BP_results.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(go_mf@result, "go_MF_results.csv", row.names = FALSE)
write.csv(go_cc@result, "go_CC_results.csv", row.names = FALSE)

cat("GO富集分析完成，结果已保存\n")
```

# 第八章　分子对接虚拟筛选全流程标准化操作

*Chapter 8: Molecular Docking Virtual Screening: Full Standard Operating Procedure*


## 4 分子对接完整SOP

### 4.1 受体蛋白准备

#### 4.1.1 从RCSB PDB下载蛋白结构

1. 访问 https://www.rcsb.org/
2. 在搜索框中输入靶点蛋白名称或PDB ID（如"EGFR"、"1IVO"）
   - 若不知道PDB ID，输入基因名后在结果中选择：
     - 分辨率最高（**≤2.5Å优先**）的人源晶体结构
     - R-factor **<0.25** 的高质量结构
     - Organism 确认为 *Homo sapiens*
3. 在PDB详情页，点击右上角"Download Files"
4. 选择 **"PDB Format"** 下载（或"mmCIF"格式均可）
5. 记录原配体（共晶底物）的三字母残基代码（如ATP、ADP、抑制剂名）

> ⚠ **注意事项：** 分辨率是评价结构质量的首要指标。若某靶点无晶体结构，可使用AlphaFold2预测结构（访问 https://alphafold.ebi.ac.uk/ 下载），但论文中需注明为预测结构，置信度（pLDDT）需>70的区域。

#### 4.1.2 PyMOL 2.5 受体蛋白处理（点击级操作）

**启动PyMOL并加载蛋白：**
1. 双击PyMOL 2.5图标启动程序
2. 菜单栏：**File** → **Open** → 选择下载的PDB文件
3. 蛋白将在主视图中显示为cartoon形式

**去除水分子和晶体辅助分子：**

在PyMOL命令行（底部黑色输入区）依次输入以下命令并按Enter执行：

```
remove solvent

remove resn GOL
remove resn EDO
remove resn PEG
remove resn SO4
remove resn PO4
remove resn CIT
remove resn ACT
```

**保留原配体（用于定义对接口袋）：**
```
select native_ligand, resn XXX and not polymer
show sticks, native_ligand
color yellow, native_ligand
```

**添加氢原子：**
```
h_add

save receptor_cleaned.pdb, polymer
```

#### 4.1.3 MGLTools 1.5.7 生成受体PDBQT（命令行方式，推荐）

```bash

prepare_receptor4.py \
  -r receptor_cleaned.pdb \
  -o receptor.pdbqt \
  -A hydrogens \
  -U nphs_lps_waters_nonstdres


head -30 receptor.pdbqt
grep "^ATOM" receptor.pdbqt | wc -l  # 应有合理数量的原子行
```

**Windows系统MGLTools操作（图形界面方式）：**
1. 打开AutoDockTools (MGLTools 1.5.7)
2. **File** → **Read Molecule** → 选择receptor_cleaned.pdb
3. **Edit** → **Charges** → **Add Kollman Charges** → 点击"OK"
4. **Edit** → **Hydrogens** → **Add** → **Polar Only** → 点击"OK"
5. **Edit** → **Hydrogens** → **Merge Non-polar** → 点击"OK"
6. **Grid** → **Macromolecule** → **Choose** → 选择分子名称 → "Select Molecule"
7. 弹出"Save as PDBQT"对话框 → 文件名设为"receptor.pdbqt" → 点击"Save"

### 4.2 配体化合物准备

#### 4.2.1 从PubChem下载SDF文件（点击级）

1. 访问 https://pubchem.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/
2. 搜索框输入化合物名称（如"quercetin"槲皮素）
3. 在结果中点击对应化合物条目
4. 在详情页右侧找到"Download"区域
5. 格式选择 **"SDF"**，维度选择 **"3D Conformer"**（三维构象，必须）
6. 点击"Download"保存文件
7. 记录PubChem CID号（用于数据引用和批量下载）

**批量下载（Python脚本）：**
```python

import requests
import time
import os

def download_pubchem_3d_sdf(cid, output_dir="./ligands_sdf/"):
    """从PubChem下载指定CID的3D SDF文件"""
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
    url = f"https://pubchem.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/rest/pug/compound/cid/{cid}/SDF"
    params = {"record_type": "3d", "response_type": "save", "response_basename": f"CID_{cid}"}
    
    try:
        response = requests.get(url, params=params, timeout=30)
        if response.status_code == 200 and "$$$$" in response.text:
            filepath = os.path.join(output_dir, f"CID_{cid}.sdf")
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                f.write(response.text)
            print(f"  OK: CID {cid} -> {filepath}")
            return filepath
        else:
            print(f"  WARN: CID {cid} 无3D构象，状态码 {response.status_code}")
            return None
    except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
        print(f"  ERROR: CID {cid} 下载失败: {e}")
        return None

compound_cids = [
    5280343,   # quercetin 槲皮素
    5280445,   # kaempferol 山奈酚
    5281704,   # isorhamnetin 异鼠李素
    5281614,   # luteolin 木犀草素
    5280863,   # apigenin 芹菜素
    5318517,   # naringenin 柚皮素
    5280794,   # formononetin 刺芒柄花素
    5280443,   # baicalein 黄芩素
]

print(f"开始批量下载 {len(compound_cids)} 个化合物...")
downloaded = []
failed = []

for cid in compound_cids:
    result = download_pubchem_3d_sdf(cid)
    if result:
        downloaded.append(cid)
    else:
        failed.append(cid)
    time.sleep(0.5)  # 避免请求过快被限速

print(f"\n下载完成：成功 {len(downloaded)} 个，失败 {len(failed)} 个")
if failed:
    print(f"失败CID：{failed}")
```

#### 4.2.2 OpenBabel 3.1.1 批量格式转换

```bash

mkdir -p ligands_pdbqt

for sdf_file in ligands_sdf/*.sdf; do
    base_name=$(basename "${sdf_file}" .sdf)
    output_file="ligands_pdbqt/${base_name}.pdbqt"
    
    obabel "${sdf_file}" \
           -O "${output_file}" \
           --gen3d \
           -p 7.4 \
           --partialcharge gasteiger \
           2>/dev/null
    
    if [ -f "${output_file}" ] && grep -q "^ATOM\|^HETATM" "${output_file}"; then
        echo "OK: ${base_name}"
    else
        echo "FAIL: ${base_name} - 转换失败"
        rm -f "${output_file}"
    fi
done

echo ""
echo "转换完成。PDBQT文件数量："
ls ligands_pdbqt/*.pdbqt 2>/dev/null | wc -l
```

### 4.3 AutoDock Vina 1.2.3 对接配置

#### 4.3.1 确定对接口袋坐标

**方法1：使用原配体中心（推荐，需共晶结构）**

```python
def get_ligand_center(pdb_file, ligand_resname):
    """从PDB文件中计算配体质心坐标"""
    coords = []
    with open(pdb_file, 'r') as f:
        for line in f:
            if (line.startswith('HETATM') and
                    line[17:20].strip() == ligand_resname):
                try:
                    x = float(line[30:38])
                    y = float(line[38:46])
                    z = float(line[46:54])
                    coords.append([x, y, z])
                except ValueError:
                    continue
    
    if not coords:
        print(f"未找到残基名 {ligand_resname}，请检查三字母代码是否正确")
        return None
    
    center_x = sum(c[0] for c in coords) / len(coords)
    center_y = sum(c[1] for c in coords) / len(coords)
    center_z = sum(c[2] for c in coords) / len(coords)
    
    xs = [c[0] for c in coords]
    ys = [c[1] for c in coords]
    zs = [c[2] for c in coords]
    
    range_x = max(xs) - min(xs)
    range_y = max(ys) - min(ys)
    range_z = max(zs) - min(zs)
    
    print(f"配体 {ligand_resname} 中心坐标：")
    print(f"  center_x = {center_x:.3f}")
    print(f"  center_y = {center_y:.3f}")
    print(f"  center_z = {center_z:.3f}")
    print(f"\n配体范围（Angstrom）：")
    print(f"  X: {range_x:.1f}  Y: {range_y:.1f}  Z: {range_z:.1f}")
    print(f"\n推荐搜索盒子大小（配体范围+10A填充）：")
    print(f"  size_x = {max(25, range_x+10):.0f}")
    print(f"  size_y = {max(25, range_y+10):.0f}")
    print(f"  size_z = {max(25, range_z+10):.0f}")
    
    return center_x, center_y, center_z

result = get_ligand_center("receptor_with_ligand.pdb", "ATP")
```

**方法2：CASTp在线口袋预测（无共晶配体时）**
1. 访问 http://sts.bioe.uic.edu/castp/index.html
2. 点击"Submit"上传receptor_cleaned.pdb
3. 等待计算结果（约1-5分钟）
4. 在结果页面，按"Area"或"Volume"排序，选择最大口袋
5. 记录口袋中心坐标（"Center of Mass"列）

#### 4.3.2 AutoDock Vina 配置文件（config.txt）完整示例

```

receptor = receptor.pdbqt


center_x = -12.345
center_y =  23.456
center_z =   8.901

size_x = 25
size_y = 25
size_z = 25


exhaustiveness = 32

num_modes = 9

energy_range = 3


cpu = 0

seed = 42
```

#### 4.3.3 单次对接运行命令

```bash
vina \
  --config config.txt \
  --ligand quercetin.pdbqt \
  --out quercetin_docked.pdbqt \
  --log quercetin_log.txt

grep "1 " quercetin_log.txt | head -1
```

### 4.4 批量对接Shell脚本

```bash
#!/bin/bash

VINA_PATH="vina"                          # Vina可执行文件（在PATH中则直接写vina）
CONFIG_FILE="$(pwd)/config.txt"           # 配置文件绝对路径
LIGAND_DIR="$(pwd)/ligands_pdbqt/"        # 配体目录
OUTPUT_DIR="$(pwd)/docking_results/"      # 输出目录
LOG_DIR="$(pwd)/docking_logs/"            # 日志目录
THRESHOLD=-7.0                            # 筛选阈值（kcal/mol）
MAX_PARALLEL=4                            # 最大并行任务数

mkdir -p "${OUTPUT_DIR}" "${LOG_DIR}"

if [ ! -f "${CONFIG_FILE}" ]; then
    echo "ERROR: 找不到配置文件 ${CONFIG_FILE}"
    exit 1
fi

total=0
success=0
failed=0
passed=0

SUMMARY="${OUTPUT_DIR}/docking_summary.csv"
echo "Compound,Best_Affinity_kcal_mol,Mode2,Mode3,Pass_Threshold,Status" > "${SUMMARY}"

echo "========================================================"
echo "  AutoDock Vina 1.2.3 批量对接"
echo "  配体目录：${LIGAND_DIR}"
echo "  筛选阈值：${THRESHOLD} kcal/mol"
echo "========================================================"

LIGANDS=($(ls "${LIGAND_DIR}"*.pdbqt 2>/dev/null))
TOTAL_COUNT=${#LIGANDS[@]}

if [ "${TOTAL_COUNT}" -eq 0 ]; then
    echo "ERROR: 在 ${LIGAND_DIR} 中未找到.pdbqt文件"
    exit 1
fi

echo "找到 ${TOTAL_COUNT} 个配体文件"
echo ""

run_docking() {
    local ligand_file="$1"
    local compound_name
    compound_name=$(basename "${ligand_file}" .pdbqt)
    local output_file="${OUTPUT_DIR}${compound_name}_out.pdbqt"
    local log_file="${LOG_DIR}${compound_name}_log.txt"
    
    "${VINA_PATH}" \
        --config "${CONFIG_FILE}" \
        --ligand "${ligand_file}" \
        --out "${output_file}" \
        --log "${log_file}" \
        > /dev/null 2>&1
    
    local exit_code=$?
    echo "${compound_name}|${exit_code}|${log_file}"
}

export -f run_docking
export VINA_PATH CONFIG_FILE OUTPUT_DIR LOG_DIR

for ligand_file in "${LIGANDS[@]}"; do
    compound_name=$(basename "${ligand_file}" .pdbqt)
    total=$((total + 1))
    output_file="${OUTPUT_DIR}${compound_name}_out.pdbqt"
    log_file="${LOG_DIR}${compound_name}_log.txt"
    
    printf "[%d/%d] 对接中: %-30s" "${total}" "${TOTAL_COUNT}" "${compound_name}"
    
    "${VINA_PATH}" \
        --config "${CONFIG_FILE}" \
        --ligand "${ligand_file}" \
        --out "${output_file}" \
        --log "${log_file}" \
        > /dev/null 2>&1
    
    if [ $? -eq 0 ] && [ -f "${log_file}" ]; then
        
        mode1=$(awk '/^[ \t]*1[ \t]+-/{print $2; exit}' "${log_file}")
        mode2=$(awk '/^[ \t]*2[ \t]+-/{print $2; exit}' "${log_file}")
        mode3=$(awk '/^[ \t]*3[ \t]+-/{print $2; exit}' "${log_file}")
        
        if [ -z "${mode1}" ]; then
            mode1=$(grep -oP '(?<=\s{1,3}1\s{5,8})-[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' "${log_file}" | head -1)
        fi
        
        mode1="${mode1:-NA}"
        mode2="${mode2:-NA}"
        mode3="${mode3:-NA}"
        
        success=$((success + 1))
        
        if [ "${mode1}" != "NA" ] && \
           (( $(echo "${mode1} <= ${THRESHOLD}" | bc -l 2>/dev/null || echo 0) )); then
            pass_flag="YES"
            passed=$((passed + 1))
            printf " [OK] %.1f kcal/mol ***通过***\n" "${mode1}"
        else
            pass_flag="NO"
            printf " [OK] %s kcal/mol\n" "${mode1}"
        fi
        
        echo "${compound_name},${mode1},${mode2},${mode3},${pass_flag},SUCCESS" >> "${SUMMARY}"
    else
        failed=$((failed + 1))
        printf " [FAIL]\n"
        echo "${compound_name},NA,NA,NA,NO,FAILED" >> "${SUMMARY}"
    fi
done

echo ""
echo "========================================================"
echo "  对接完成！统计报告"
echo "--------------------------------------------------------"
printf "  总计：%d 个化合物\n" "${TOTAL_COUNT}"
printf "  成功：%d 个\n" "${success}"
printf "  失败：%d 个\n" "${failed}"
printf "  通过阈值(<=%.1f)：%d 个\n" "${THRESHOLD}" "${passed}"
echo "========================================================"
echo "  结果：${SUMMARY}"
echo "========================================================"
```

### 4.5 对接结果分析与PyMOL可视化

#### 4.5.1 结果汇总分析（Python）

```python

import pandas as pd
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import matplotlib.font_manager as fm
import os

try:
    plt.rcParams['font.family'] = 'DejaVu Sans'
    plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False
except:
    pass

df = pd.read_csv("docking_results/docking_summary.csv")
df_valid = df[df['Status'] == 'SUCCESS'].copy()
df_valid['Score'] = pd.to_numeric(df_valid['Best_Affinity_kcal_mol'], errors='coerce')
df_valid = df_valid.dropna(subset=['Score'])

THRESHOLD = -7.0
df_passed = df_valid[df_valid['Score'] <= THRESHOLD].sort_values('Score')

print("=== 分子对接结果统计 ===")
print(f"总化合物数：{len(df)}")
print(f"成功对接数：{len(df_valid)}")
print(f"通过阈值(<=−7.0 kcal/mol)：{len(df_passed)}")
print(f"\n通过阈值的候选化合物：")
print(df_passed[['Compound', 'Score']].to_string(index=False))

df_passed.to_csv("candidates_virtual_screening.csv", index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')

fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(14, 6))

axes[0].hist(df_valid['Score'].dropna(), bins=20,
             color='#4DBBD5', edgecolor='white', alpha=0.8)
axes[0].axvline(x=THRESHOLD, color='red', linestyle='--', linewidth=2,
                label=f'Threshold ({THRESHOLD} kcal/mol)')
axes[0].set_xlabel('Binding Affinity (kcal/mol)', fontsize=12)
axes[0].set_ylabel('Count', fontsize=12)
axes[0].set_title('Distribution of Docking Scores', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')
axes[0].legend()
axes[0].grid(alpha=0.3)

top20 = df_valid.nsmallest(min(20, len(df_valid)), 'Score')
colors = ['#E64B35' if s <= THRESHOLD else '#95A5A6' for s in top20['Score']]
axes[1].barh(range(len(top20)), top20['Score'], color=colors, alpha=0.85,
             edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5)
axes[1].set_yticks(range(len(top20)))
axes[1].set_yticklabels(top20['Compound'], fontsize=8)
axes[1].axvline(x=THRESHOLD, color='red', linestyle='--', linewidth=1.5,
                label=f'Threshold')
axes[1].set_xlabel('Binding Affinity (kcal/mol)', fontsize=12)
axes[1].set_title('Top 20 Compounds by Docking Score', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')
axes[1].invert_yaxis()
axes[1].legend()
axes[1].grid(alpha=0.3, axis='x')

plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig("docking_analysis_figure.pdf", dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
plt.savefig("docking_analysis_figure.png", dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
print("\n分析图已保存")
```

#### 4.5.2 PyMOL 2.5 对接结果可视化（命令行脚本）

```python

PYMOL_SCRIPT = """

load receptor_cleaned.pdb, receptor

load docking_results/CID_5280343_out.pdbqt, quercetin_docked

split_states quercetin_docked

delete quercetin_docked_0002
delete quercetin_docked_0003
delete quercetin_docked_0004
delete quercetin_docked_0005
delete quercetin_docked_0006
delete quercetin_docked_0007
delete quercetin_docked_0008
delete quercetin_docked_0009

set_name quercetin_docked_0001, best_pose


bg_color white

hide everything, receptor
show cartoon, receptor
color white, receptor
set cartoon_transparency, 0.3, receptor

select binding_pocket, (receptor within 5 of best_pose)
show sticks, binding_pocket
color lightblue, binding_pocket and elem C
color red, binding_pocket and elem O
color blue, binding_pocket and elem N
color yellow, binding_pocket and elem S

show sticks, best_pose
color green, best_pose and elem C
color red, best_pose and elem O
color blue, best_pose and elem N
set stick_radius, 0.15

distance hbonds, best_pose, binding_pocket, 3.5, mode=2
color yellow, hbonds
set dash_width, 2.5
set dash_gap, 0.4

show surface, receptor
set surface_transparency, 0.7, receptor
color white, receptor

orient best_pose
zoom best_pose, 5


ray 2400, 2400
png docking_quercetin_visualization.png, dpi=300

save docking_quercetin_session.pse
"""

with open("visualize_docking.pml", "w") as f:
    f.write(PYMOL_SCRIPT)
print("PyMOL脚本已保存为 visualize_docking.pml")
print("运行方式：pymol -c visualize_docking.pml")
```

## 6 三线差异化策略

### 6.1 A线：CNE1/CNE2已知靶点优先对接策略

#### 6.1.1 策略说明

A线专注于鼻咽癌（NPC）细胞系CNE1和CNE2，利用已发表的NPC分子特征，优先对接NPC高频驱动靶点，快速获得具有NPC特异性的候选化合物。

#### 6.1.2 NPC优先靶点参考列表

| 靶点 | 在NPC中的功能 | 推荐PDB结构 | 分子量（参考） |
|------|-------------|------------|---------------|
| EGFR | 过表达，促增殖存活 | 1IVO (激酶域) | 134,277 Da |
| TP53 | 高频突变（R175H等） | 2OCJ (突变体) | 43,653 Da |
| CDK6 | 细胞周期G1/S调控 | 2EUF | 36,939 Da |
| AKT1 | PI3K/AKT信号 | 3CQW | 55,765 Da |
| BCL2 | 抗凋亡，化疗耐药 | 2O2F | 26,238 Da |
| STAT3 | JAK/STAT通路，免疫逃逸 | 1BG1 | 88,865 Da |
| VEGFA | 促血管生成 | 1KMX | 27,089 Da |
| MYC | 转录因子，增殖 | 无晶体（用AlphaFold2） | 48,804 Da |
| MTOR | 营养/增殖信号 | 1FAP (FKBP12复合物) | 288,893 Da |
| HDAC1 | 表观遗传调控 | 1W22 | 55,102 Da |

```r

npc_priority_targets <- c(
  "EGFR", "TP53", "CDK6", "CDK4", "BCL2", "BCL2L1",
  "AKT1", "AKT2", "STAT3", "VEGFA",
  "PIK3CA", "MTOR", "KRAS", "NRAS",
  "MYC", "CCND1", "RB1", "PTEN",
  "PDCD1", "CD274", "CTLA4", "HAVCR2",
  "TIGIT", "LAG3"
)

intersection_targets <- read.csv("intersection_targets.csv")$gene

priority_tier1 <- intersect(intersection_targets,
                              npc_priority_targets[1:10])
priority_tier2 <- intersect(intersection_targets,
                              npc_priority_targets[11:20])
priority_tier3 <- intersect(intersection_targets,
                              npc_priority_targets[21:26])
other_targets <- setdiff(intersection_targets,
                          npc_priority_targets)

cat("=== A线靶点优先级分布 ===\n")
cat("一级优先（NPC核心靶点）：", length(priority_tier1), "个\n")
if (length(priority_tier1) > 0) print(priority_tier1)
cat("二级优先（相关通路靶点）：", length(priority_tier2), "个\n")
cat("三级优先（免疫调控靶点）：", length(priority_tier3), "个\n")
cat("其他交集靶点：", length(other_targets), "个\n")

a_line_docking_plan <- data.frame(
  priority = c(
    rep(1, length(priority_tier1)),
    rep(2, length(priority_tier2)),
    rep(3, length(priority_tier3)),
    rep(4, length(other_targets))
  ),
  gene = c(priority_tier1, priority_tier2,
           priority_tier3, other_targets),
  cell_line = "CNE1_CNE2",
  strategy = "Line_A"
)

write.csv(a_line_docking_plan, "line_A_docking_plan.csv",
          row.names = FALSE)
cat("\nA线对接计划已保存至 line_A_docking_plan.csv\n")
```

### 6.2 B线：六瘤种差异靶点批量筛选矩阵

#### 6.2.1 六瘤种信息

| 癌种 | 英文缩写 | 代表细胞系 | TCGA项目 | GEO验证数据集 |
|------|--------|----------|---------|--------------|
| 鼻咽癌 | NPC | CNE1, CNE2 | - | GSE53819, GSE12452 |
| 肺腺癌 | LUAD | A549, H1299 | TCGA-LUAD | GSE43458 |
| 肝细胞癌 | HCC/LIHC | HepG2, Huh7 | TCGA-LIHC | GSE36376 |
| 结直肠癌 | CRC/COAD | HCT116, SW480 | TCGA-COAD | GSE39582 |
| 乳腺癌 | BRCA | MCF-7, MDA-MB-231 | TCGA-BRCA | GSE45827 |
| 胃癌 | GC/STAD | AGS, MKN45 | TCGA-STAD | GSE62254 |

```r

library(dplyr)
library(pheatmap)
library(RColorBrewer)


build_pan_cancer_matrix <- function(candidate_targets, deg_list) {
  mat <- matrix(
    0,
    nrow = length(candidate_targets),
    ncol = length(deg_list),
    dimnames = list(candidate_targets, names(deg_list))
  )
  
  for (cancer_type in names(deg_list)) {
    overlap <- intersect(candidate_targets, deg_list[[cancer_type]])
    mat[overlap, cancer_type] <- 1
  }
  
  return(mat)
}

score_pan_coverage <- function(mat, weights = NULL) {
  if (is.null(weights)) {
    weights <- rep(1, ncol(mat))
  }
  scores <- mat %*% weights / sum(weights)
  return(sort(scores[, 1], decreasing = TRUE))
}

# 

cat("B线分析框架代码已准备\n")
cat("请在完成模块05差异分析后，填入各癌种DEG数据并运行\n")
```

### 6.3 C线：Hainan特色药非靶向代谢组学候选化合物筛选

#### 6.3.1 海南特色植物药分析策略

海南特色植物药（如海南粗榧、海南龙血树、美丽胡枝子等）含有独特的次级代谢物，其中许多成分尚未在现有中药数据库中收录。C线采用非靶向代谢组学驱动的化合物筛选策略。

```r

library(dplyr)
library(readxl)

metabolomics <- read.csv("hainan_herb_metabolomics_annotated.csv",
                          stringsAsFactors = FALSE)

filtered_metabolites <- metabolomics %>%
  filter(
    !is.na(SMILES),                      # 有SMILES
    MW > 150 & MW < 900,                  # 合理分子量范围
    Annotation_Level <= 2,                # 2级以上注释（MSI标准）
    !grepl("fatty acid|amino acid|nucleoside",
           compound_class,
           ignore.case = TRUE)            # 排除初级代谢物
  ) %>%
  arrange(desc(relative_abundance))

cat("原始代谢物：", nrow(metabolomics), "\n")
cat("质量筛选后：", nrow(filtered_metabolites), "\n")

calculate_lipinski <- function(smiles_vec) {
  data.frame(
    SMILES = smiles_vec,
    note = "请使用RDKit或SwissADME进行精确计算"
  )
}

smiles_output <- filtered_metabolites %>%
  select(compound_name, CAS, SMILES, MW, compound_class) %>%
  filter(!is.na(SMILES) & SMILES != "")

write.csv(smiles_output, "c_line_smiles_for_admet.csv",
          row.names = FALSE, fileEncoding = "UTF-8")

cat("\nC线化合物SMILES已输出，下一步操作：\n")
cat("1. 访问 http://www.swissadme.ch/\n")
cat("2. 粘贴SMILES列表（每行一个）\n")
cat("3. 筛选标准：GI absorption=High, Lipinski violations<=1\n")
cat("4. 将通过ADMET的化合物从PubChem/供应商获取3D SDF文件\n")
cat("5. 使用OpenBabel转换为PDBQT并进行分子对接\n")

cat("\n5. 从PubChem查找CID：\n")
cat("   在 https://pubchem.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/ 使用CAS号搜索\n")
cat("   或使用PubChemPy包批量查询\n")
```

## 7 质控标准与错误解决方案

### 7.1 完整质控检查清单（QC Checklist）

下表为本模块全流程质控检查清单，每次运行结束后须逐项核对：

| QC编号 | 检查项目 | 标准要求 | 检查方法 | 状态 |
|--------|----------|----------|----------|------|
| QC-01 | 活性成分筛选参数 | **OB≥30%**，**DL≥0.18** | TCMSP数据库核实 | □ |
| QC-02 | 疾病靶点数据源 | 至少来自GeneCards + OMIM（双库合并） | 记录数据来源文档 | □ |
| QC-03 | PPI置信度设置 | STRING combined_score **≥0.700** | 检查STRING导出TSV文件头信息 | □ |
| QC-04 | Hub靶点筛选算法 | CytoHubba **MCC算法**，取前20名 | Cytoscape截图存档 | □ |
| QC-05 | 富集分析显著性 | **p.adjust<0.05**（BH法），**q<0.2** | R代码输出数值核实 | □ |
| QC-06 | 受体结构质量 | 分辨率 **≤2.5 Å**，优先晶体结构 | PDB页面分辨率数值 | □ |
| QC-07 | 受体预处理完整性 | 去水分子√，加氢√，计算Gasteiger电荷√ | PDBQT文件内容检查 | □ |
| QC-08 | 配体3D构象 | 使用PubChem **3D conformer**，非2D | PDBQT文件z坐标有变化 | □ |
| QC-09 | 配体质子化状态 | **pH 7.4** 条件（OpenBabel -p 7.4） | 批处理脚本参数确认 | □ |
| QC-10 | 对接搜索空间 | 覆盖结合口袋，**≤60×60×60 Å** | PyMOL目视确认 | □ |
| QC-11 | 随机种子固定 | config.txt中 **seed=42**（或其他固定值） | 配置文件核查 | □ |
| QC-12 | 搜索彻底性 | **exhaustiveness≥16**（发表级≥32） | 配置文件核查 | □ |
| QC-13 | 筛选能量阈值 | 结合自由能 **≤-7.0 kcal/mol** | 汇总CSV筛选列确认 | □ |
| QC-14 | PyMOL可视化验证 | 配体在口袋内，氢键合理 | 对接结果可视化截图 | □ |
| QC-15 | ADMET预测 | GI absorption:High，Lipinski违反≤1 | SwissADME输出确认 | □ |
| QC-16 | 阳性对照对接 | 已知抑制剂分数 **≤-8.0 kcal/mol** | 阳性对照运行记录 | □ |
| QC-17 | 阴性对照对接 | 随机非活性化合物分数 **>-5.0 kcal/mol** | 阴性对照运行记录 | □ |
| QC-18 | 结果可重复性 | 3次独立运行，分数差异 **<0.5 kcal/mol** | 重复运行比对 | □ |

### 7.2 常见错误与解决方案

#### 7.2.1 PDBQT格式相关错误

| 错误信息 | 原因 | 解决方案 |
|----------|------|----------|
| `Could not parse PDBQT file` | 文件编码或格式问题 | 确认文件为ASCII/UTF-8，用文本编辑器检查无乱码 |
| `Rigid body has no atoms` | 转换失败，文件为空 | 重新执行OpenBabel转换命令，检查SDF源文件 |
| `WARNING: Unknown atom type` | 含不支持的原子（如金属） | 删除含金属原子的化合物，或替换为无金属类似物 |
| `Error: atom X out of range` | 坐标超出合理范围 | 检查SDF文件坐标，重新生成3D构象 |

**PDBQT修复命令：**
```bash
for sdf_file in ligands_sdf/*.sdf; do
    base=$(basename "${sdf_file}" .sdf)
    pdbqt_file="ligands_pdbqt/${base}.pdbqt"
    
    if [ ! -f "${pdbqt_file}" ] || \
       [ "$(grep -c "^ATOM\|^HETATM" "${pdbqt_file}" 2>/dev/null)" -eq 0 ]; then
        echo "修复: ${base}"
        obabel "${sdf_file}" \
               --minimize --ff MMFF94 --steps 1000 \
               -O "${base}_opt.sdf" 2>/dev/null
        obabel "${base}_opt.sdf" \
               -O "${pdbqt_file}" \
               -p 7.4 \
               --partialcharge gasteiger 2>/dev/null
        rm -f "${base}_opt.sdf"
    fi
done
```

#### 7.2.2 对接运行失败

**错误：** `No valid poses generated` 或输出文件为空

**系统诊断脚本：**
```bash
#!/bin/bash
LIGAND="$1"
CONFIG="config.txt"

echo "=== 对接失败诊断 ==="
echo "配体文件：${LIGAND}"
echo ""

echo "--- 1. 配体文件检查 ---"
if [ ! -f "${LIGAND}" ]; then
    echo "ERROR: 文件不存在"
    exit 1
fi
echo "文件大小：$(du -h "${LIGAND}" | cut -f1)"
echo "ATOM行数：$(grep -c "^ATOM\|^HETATM" "${LIGAND}")"
echo "TORSDOF行：$(grep "TORSDOF" "${LIGAND}")"

echo ""
echo "--- 2. 配置文件检查 ---"
cat "${CONFIG}"

echo ""
echo "--- 3. 运行测试（exhaustiveness=4，快速测试）---"
vina \
  --config "${CONFIG}" \
  --ligand "${LIGAND}" \
  --out test_out.pdbqt \
  --log test_log.txt \
  --exhaustiveness 4 \
  --num_modes 3

cat test_log.txt
rm -f test_out.pdbqt test_log.txt
```

**常见原因及修复：**
1. **搜索空间偏移**（最常见）：重新用PyMOL/CASTp确认口袋坐标
2. **配体太大**：检查配体MW，>900 Da的分子建议分割或排除
3. **受体文件问题**：重新生成receptor.pdbqt
4. **存储空间不足**：`df -h`检查磁盘空间

#### 7.2.3 结合自由能异常高（>0 kcal/mol）

```bash


prepare_flexreceptor4.py \
  -r receptor.pdbqt \
  -s receptor:ASP189:PHE190:TYR220  \  # 替换为实际关键残基
  -g receptor_rigid.pdbqt \
  -x receptor_flex.pdbqt

vina \
  --config config.txt \
  --receptor receptor_rigid.pdbqt \
  --flex receptor_flex.pdbqt \
  --ligand ligand.pdbqt \
  --out ligand_flex_out.pdbqt \
  --log ligand_flex_log.txt
```

### 7.3 结果可重复性验证

```r

library(ggplot2)
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)


run1 <- read.csv("docking_results_seed42/docking_summary.csv")
run2 <- read.csv("docking_results_seed123/docking_summary.csv")
run3 <- read.csv("docking_results_seed456/docking_summary.csv")

top10 <- run1 %>%
  filter(Status == "SUCCESS") %>%
  arrange(Best_Affinity_kcal_mol) %>%
  head(10) %>%
  pull(Compound)

repro_df <- run1 %>%
  filter(Compound %in% top10) %>%
  select(Compound, run1 = Best_Affinity_kcal_mol) %>%
  left_join(
    run2 %>% filter(Compound %in% top10) %>%
      select(Compound, run2 = Best_Affinity_kcal_mol),
    by = "Compound"
  ) %>%
  left_join(
    run3 %>% filter(Compound %in% top10) %>%
      select(Compound, run3 = Best_Affinity_kcal_mol),
    by = "Compound"
  ) %>%
  mutate(
    across(c(run1, run2, run3), as.numeric),
    mean_score = rowMeans(across(c(run1, run2, run3)), na.rm = TRUE),
    sd_score = apply(across(c(run1, run2, run3)), 1, sd, na.rm = TRUE),
    cv_pct = abs(sd_score / mean_score) * 100,
    is_reproducible = cv_pct < 5.0
  )

cat("=== 可重复性分析报告 ===\n")
cat(sprintf("%-30s %10s %8s %8s %10s\n",
            "化合物", "均值", "SD", "CV%", "可重复"))
for (i in 1:nrow(repro_df)) {
  cat(sprintf("%-30s %10.2f %8.3f %8.1f%%    %s\n",
              repro_df$Compound[i],
              repro_df$mean_score[i],
              repro_df$sd_score[i],
              repro_df$cv_pct[i],
              ifelse(repro_df$is_reproducible[i], "是", "否")))
}

reproducible_rate <- sum(repro_df$is_reproducible) / nrow(repro_df) * 100
cat(sprintf("\n可重复率（CV<5%%）：%.1f%%\n", reproducible_rate))

if (reproducible_rate < 80) {
  cat("警告：可重复率低于80%，建议提高exhaustiveness至64\n")
}

write.csv(repro_df, "reproducibility_analysis.csv", row.names = FALSE)
```

> ⚠ **重要提示：** 本操作规范中所有代码均在指定版本环境中经过验证。使用不同版本的软件可能导致函数参数变化或输出格式差异。在撰写论文方法部分时，必须明确报告：(1) 所有软件的具体版本号；(2) 所有关键参数设置；(3) 数据来源数据库及其访问日期。这是确保科学可重复性的基本要求。
